Wato, za a yi kuka a duniya; za a yi hargitsi a ko'ina. Babu wanda zai iya fahimtar waɗannan sai masu ibada. A cikin waɗannan abubuwan, miyagu da masu zunubi marasa adadi za a halaka su. Mahapurush ya ce waɗannan abubuwan za su faru a cikin lokacin shekaru 2.5 na Meena-Shani Yoga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta cewa lokacin da Saturn yake a Pisces, a lokacin, za a yi gwaje-gwaje ga dukkan munafukai da marasa ibada a Jobra da Kaliaboda na Cuttack. Uwaye Masu Tsarki (Alloli Mata) za su halaka dukkan irin waɗannan marasa ibada. A lokacin, za a kuma yi duhu na kwana bakwai da dare bakwai. Hankalin kowa zai daina aiki. A lokacin, masu zunubi ba za su ma iya samun abinci ba.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Saturn yake a Pisces, za a yi hargitsi a Indiya. A lokacin, Ubangiji Maɗaukaki zai bar siffarsa ta ɗan adam ta yau da kullun kuma zai ɗauki siffar Kalki mai matuƙar iko.

A wani littafin Malika, Mahapurush ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a lokacin haɗuwar Meena-Shani, za a sake samun mummunan narkarwa (Pralaya) a Duniya.

Achyutananda Das ji ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a lokacin da Saturn ya wuce ta Pisces, Yaƙin Duniya na Uku zai fara a Duniya, ciki har da Indiya. Na tsawon shekaru biyu da rabi, za a ga mummunar halaka a Duniya. Dukkan duniya za a halaka ta kuma za ta ɗauki siffar wurin ƙona gawarwaki. A lokacin, mutane marasa adadi za su mutu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a wani littafin Malika mai suna ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Ma'ana:
Wato, ya ku mutane, ku yi hankali. Meena-Shani Yoga yana zuwa. A lokacin, bala'o'i da yawa za su zo, kuma halakar za ta shafi kusan kowa; ba za a bar kowa ba. Hankalin mutane zai daina aiki. Hargitsi zai mamaye ko'ina.

An haife shi a ƙarni na 19, mawallafin waliyi Bhima Bhoi ya rubuta game da wannan batu a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Padmakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Ma'ana:
Wato, bayan tsakiyar Meena-Shani Yoga, koguna da rafuka za su fara bushewa nan take. Sojojin ƙasashe maƙiya za su shiga Odisha su yi yaƙi. A tsakiyar wannan Meena-Shani Yoga, lokacin da Mars yake a Leo, girgizar ƙasa ma za ta faru. Ubangiji zai kiyaye masu ibadarsa lafiya da kulawa a cikin kogon duwatsu. Chandi da Yoginis za su halaka miyagu da masu zunubi.

Mawallafin mai ibada Narada Das ya kuma ambaci hargitsin da ke faruwa a lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga a cikin littafinsa na Malika. Ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Ma'ana:
Wato, Meena-Shani Yoga zai ɗauki tsawon shekaru biyu da rabi kuma a lokacin, mutane marasa adadi za su mutu. Za a yi kuka a Duniya. Yoginis za su gudanar da aikin halaka. A wannan lokacin, gawarwaki za su bazu a faɗin ƙasa. Duniya za ta girgiza da rurin Bhairavi.

Yana ambato tattaunawar tsakanin Ubangiji Krishna da Uwar Rukmini a lokacin Dvapara Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Rukmini Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a lokacin da Saturn ya wuce a Pisces, za a yi mummunan yaƙi a Indiya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya sake rubutawa:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Meena-Shani Yoga ya faru, za a ga mummunan hargitsi a wannan Duniya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafin Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, a lokacin kafa Dharma, za a yi kwana goma sha uku (Paksha). Za a yi Meena-Shani Yoga wanda zai ɗauki tsawon shekaru biyu da rabi. A lokacin, taurari za su bayyana da rana. A wannan lokacin, masu ibada za su fuskanci matsaloli da yawa. Za a yi yaƙi a Choudwar na Cuttack; kayan aikin kimiyya da lantarki za su daina aiki. Za a yi mummunan yaƙi. Makamai masu linzami na zamani da manyan bindigogi na ƙasashe maƙiya za a fuskance su da ikon tuba (Tapa) na waliyyai da masu hikima na Indiya. Ubangiji Kalki zai bayyana; masu ibada za su sami hangen nesa (Darshan) na Ubangiji. Zamanin Madawwami (Ananta Yuga) zai zo. Burin Achyutananda zai cika.

Ya sake rubutawa a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Ma'ana:
Wato, taurari za su fado daga sama da rana; mutane marasa adadi za su mutu saboda tasirin cututtuka da annoba. A wannan lokacin, Meena-Shani Yoga zai ci gaba.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, lokacin halaka ga bil'adama zai fara. Tsarin dimokuradiyya zai rushe, kuma Indiya za ta rasa 'yancinta—yana iya ma shiga karkashin mulkin wani. Rikici zai mamaye tsarin gudanarwa, kuma kasar za ta fada cikin rudani. Masu ibada za su firgita idan suka ga waɗannan abubuwan. Wutar halaka za ta mamaye ba kawai Odisha da Indiya ba, har ma da dukan duniya.

Achyutananda ji ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Ma'ana:
Wato, don 'yantar da Duniya daga zunubi, Ubangiji Mahavishnu zai bayyana a Duniya. A lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga, yana ɗaukar siffar Kalki, Ubangiji Maɗaukaki zai yi tafiya a cikin ƙasar kuma ta hanyar halaka miyagu da masu zunubi, zai 'yantar da Uwar Duniya.

Mahapurush ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Fassarar Harafi:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Ma'ana:
Wato, sauran yakin Mahabharata zai faru. Za a yi mummunan yaƙi a Virat Gadi ko Choudwar na Cuttack, kuma takobi mai hannu goma sha biyu zai kashe masu zunubi. Za a yi girgizar ƙasa mai tsanani wanda zai halaka miyagu. Za a yi gwaje-gwaje ga dukan munafukai a Jobra Ghat na Cuttack, kuma Uwar Ubangiji za ta sha jininsu. A wannan lokacin, za a kare masu ibada. Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa waɗannan abubuwan za su faru a Meena-Shani Yoga.

Achyutananda Das ji ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Tattvabodhini’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Ma'ana:
Wato, bayan shigar Saturn cikin Pisces, jihar Odisha za ta lalace.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya sake rubutawa a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Ma'ana:
Wato, Meena-Shani Yoga zai faru, kuma za a yi kwana goma sha uku. A daidai wannan lokacin, mutane za su mutu a asirce, kuma Dharma za a kafa.

Bugu da ƙari a cikin ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga, Kaliyuga zai ƙare kuma Dharma za a kafa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya sake rubutawa a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Ma'ana:
Wato, wannan duniya za ta "lalace" ko kuma ta halaka gaba ɗaya. Uwar Bhairavi za ta fitar da wata tsawa mai ban tsoro; masu zunubi da yawa za su fuskanci mutuwa ba zato ba tsammani, kuma da yawa za su mutu a asirce. Duk waɗannan ayyukan halaka za su faru a lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga, ma'ana lokacin da Saturn yake a cikin alamar zodiac na Pisces.

Daga ayoyin Malika da aka bayar a sama, a bayyane yake cewa a lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga, ayyukan halaka na Ubangiji Kalki za su fi tasiri. A wannan lokacin, dukan Duniya za ta shaida manyan abubuwan bala'i da ba a taɓa gani ba. Za a ga kuka, yaƙi, rikice-rikice, hargitsi, da bala'o'i na halitta a ko'ina. A wannan lokacin, za a halaka miyagu da marasa adalci daga dukan Duniya.

5.2 A wane Meena-Shani ne Leelas na Ubangiji Kalki za su faru?

Bayan sanin daga ayoyin da ke sama cewa Kalki destruction-leelas za su faru a lokacin Meena-Shani, babban tambaya ta taso: a wane takamaiman lokacin Meena-Shani ne waɗannan abubuwan za su faru?

Meena-Shani Yoga yana faruwa sau ɗaya a kowace shekara 30. Saboda haka, yana da muhimmanci a san wane Meena-Shani, bisa ga Malika, zai shaida abubuwan kafa Dharma.

Bayan bincike mai zurfi, mun gano cewa Saturn yana cikin Pisces a 1995; a halin yanzu, a 2025, Saturn ya sake shiga Pisces; sannan a 2054, kuma kimanin shekaru 30 bayan haka, a 2084, wucewar Saturn a Pisces zai sake faruwa.

Wane Meena-Shani?

An rubuta a sarari a cikin Bhavishya Malika cewa lokacin da waɗannan abubuwan suka faru a cikin shekaru biyu da rabi na Meena-Shani, sunan Sarkin Puri, Odisha, a wannan lokacin zai zama Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Saturn yake a Pisces, a wannan lokacin, duhu mai zurfi zai rufe Duniya na kwanaki 7 da dare 7. Rushewar da abubuwa biyar (Kasa, Ruwa, Wuta, Iska, da Ether) suka haifar zai cinye dukan Duniya. Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa a wannan lokacin, sunan Sarkin Puri zai zama ‘Divyasingha’.

Yanzu akwai wasu abubuwa da suka cancanci a lura da su a nan. Sarkin Puri na yanzu, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV), yana kimanin shekaru 72 a yanzu. A Meena-Shani na gaba—wato, shekaru 30 daga yanzu—shekarunsa za su zama 100+ kuma akwai kusan yiwuwar sifili cewa zai kasance yana kan gadon sarautar Puri a wannan lokacin!

Yanzu wasu mutane, waɗanda suka bayyana lokacin abubuwan a matsayin Meena-Shani na gaba (wato, shekarar 2054–2057), suna ba da shawarar cewa yana iya faruwa cewa waɗannan abubuwan za su faru a lokacin mulkin wani sarki mai suna Divyasingha! Amma bari in gaya muku cewa wannan ba zai yiwu ba. Sarkin Puri na yanzu shine Sarki na 19, kuma bisa ga Malika, Sarki na 19 zai zama Sarki na ƙarshe na Kaliyuga. Wato, bisa ga Malika, bayan Sarkin yanzu Shri Divyasingha Deva, ba za a sake samun Sarakunan Puri ba.

Tattaunawar da ke sama ta tabbatar da cewa dukan abubuwan bala'i za su faru a lokacin Meena-Shani Yoga tsakanin 2025–2028, a lokacin mulkin Shri Divyasingha Deva IV.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya sake rubutawa a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a matakin ƙarshe na Kaliyuga, za a yi rashin adalci mai yawa da kuma nauyin zunubi mai yawa. A wannan lokacin, ruwan teku zai shiga Haikalin Jagannath. Yaƙi zai barke a cikin haikalin. Jini zai gudana ta Ƙofar Zaki (Singhadwara). A wannan lokacin, duhu zai rufe dukan Duniya na kwanaki bakwai da dare bakwai. Duk waɗanda suke masu ibada na munafunci, waɗanda suke samun abincinsu ta hanyar ayyukan mugunta, Uwaye Masu Tsarki za su halaka su. Masu ibada za su ciyar da lokacinsu cikin farin ciki. A wannan lokacin, Sarkin Puri zai zama Shri Divyasingha Deva, wanda azzalumai za su kama ko su sace shi.

A bayyane yake daga ayar da ke sama cewa lokacin da abubuwan kafa Dharma suka faru, kuma lokacin da akwai duhu na kwanaki bakwai da dare bakwai a Duniya, Sarkin Puri a wannan lokacin zai zama Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Wani Mahapurush daga Odisha da aka haifa a ƙarni na 20 ya rubuta a cikin ɗaya daga cikin littafinsa cewa bayan shekarar 2033, hasken sabon zamani (Satyayuga) zai bayyana; ma'ana, bisa ga Mahapurush, dukan leelas na canjin zamanai za a kammala su kafin shekarar 2033. Za mu gabatar da ayoyin da suka shafi wannan a gabanku a Babi na 12. Ma'anar ita ce dukan abubuwan bala'i za su faru kafin 2033.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (makaho mai ibada-mawaƙi da aka haifa a Sambalpur, Odisha a ƙarni na 19) ya rubuta game da wannan batu a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Padma Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, destruction-leelas na Ubangiji Kalki za su faru. Wannan lokacin zai ɗauki shekaru biyu da rabi. A wannan lokacin, za a yi yaƙe-yaƙe marasa adadi tsakanin ƙasashe daban-daban. Ubangiji Kalki zai kare masu ibada, kuma Uwar Durga za ta halaka miyagu da masu zunubi.

A cikin ayar da ke sama, Mahapurush ya bayyana a sarari a wane Meena-Shani ne Kalki destruction-leelas za su faru. Yana bayyana asalin wannan Meena-Shani, ya ce a ranar da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, taurari 5 za su riga sun kasance a Pisces. Wato, a wannan ranar, jimillar taurari 6 za su kasance a Pisces—taurari 5 da suka riga sun kasance da Saturn ɗaya, jimillar taurari 6.

Idan muka duba Meena-Shani na 2025, mun gano cewa maganar Mahapurush tana tabbatar da zama daidai. A ranar 29 ga Maris 2025, ranar da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, taurari biyar (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Rana, da Wata) sun riga sun kasance a Pisces.

Yanzu, idan muka duba Meena-Shani na 2054, mun gano cewa a ranar 14 ga Mayu 2054, ranar da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, taurari 2 kawai (Venus da Mars) za su kasance a Pisces a baya, kuma tare da Saturn, sun zama taurari 3. Alhali kuwa bisa ga Saint Bhima Bhoi, ya kamata a sami jimillar taurari 6 a Pisces, ba 3 ba.

Idan muka duba Meena-Shani Yoga na shekarar 2084, mun gano cewa a ranar 20 ga Maris 2084, ranar da Saturn ya shiga Pisces, taurari 2 kawai (Mercury da Rana) za su kasance a Pisces a baya. Ko da tare da Saturn, taurari 3 ne. Wannan yana nufin cewa wucewar taurari 6 ba ta faruwa a nan ma.

A bayyane yake cewa Mahapurush ba ya magana game da Meena-Shani Yoga na 2054 ko 2084. Meena-Shani da yake magana a cikin littafinsa na Malika mai suna ‘Padma Kalpa’ shine Meena-Shani na 2025.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya kuma bayyana a cikin littafinsa na Malika wanda zai zama Mahant (Shugaba) na Padmaban Peeth ɗinsa da ke Nemalo, Cuttack, lokacin da waɗannan destruction-leelas suka faru. Ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Ma'ana:
Wato, ya almajiri Ramadas, lokacin da Kaliyuga ya ƙare, a wannan lokacin wani mutum mai suna Gaganānanda zai zama Shugaban Peeth ɗina.

Mahapurush ya rubuta a wani rubutun Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Fassarar Harafi:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Ma'ana:
Wato, ayyukan Ubangiji za su faru a lokacin Mahant Gaganānanda. A wancan lokacin, za a yi kuka a jihar Odisha.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Abin lura ne cewa a cikin tsara ta 14 na Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, zuriyarsa kuma mai shekaru 85–90 a halin yanzu Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji shine Shugaban wurin zama na ruhaniya na Mahapurush, Padmaban, Nemalo. Wannan kuma ya bayyana cewa Mahapurush yana magana ne game da lokacin yanzu.

Daga abubuwa huɗu da ke sama, a bayyane yake cewa bisa ga Malika, Meena-Shani wanda ayyukan halaka na Kalki za su faru zai zo kafin shekarar 2033, kuma a lokacin Meena-Shani, Shri Divyasingha Deva zai zama Sarkin Puri kuma Shri Gaganānanda zai zama Mahant na Padmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Ba wai kawai wannan ba, amma a ranar da Saturn zai shiga Pisces, Shad-graha (taurari 6) Yoga zai kasance yana samuwa a Pisces.

A bayyane yake cewa Mahapurush yana nuni ne ga Meena-Shani da zai zo tsakanin 2025–2028 saboda dukkan abubuwa huɗu da ke sama sun shafi wannan Meena-Shani Yoga kawai kuma ba wani ba.

Babu shakka cewa bisa ga Malika, manyan abubuwan bala'i da Ubangiji Kalki zai aiwatar za su faru tsakanin 2025–2028.

Lura: A kwanakin nan wasu mutane suna cewa Meena-Shani da aka ambata a cikin Malika ba Meena-Shani na yanzu ba ne, amma na gaba ne da zai zo a 2054! Zai dace ga dukkan masu ibada da ke da imani a cikin Malika kada su ruɗe da wannan, saboda irin waɗannan maganganun ba daidai ba ne gaba ɗaya.

6 Sarkin Puri a Ƙarshen Kaliyuga

A babi na baya, mun ga cewa dukkan manyan abubuwan kafa Dharma za su faru a lokacin mulkin Sarkin Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. A wannan babi, za mu fahimci wannan ta hanyar wasu ayoyin Malika.

Ɗaya daga cikin Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Kali Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, farawa daga Sarki Purushottama Deva bayan Sarki Indradyumna, jimillar sarakuna 19 za su zama Sarakunan Jagannath Puri a lokuta daban-daban. Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa Sarki na 19 zai kasance ba shi da ɗa kuma shi ne zai zama sarki na ƙarshe; ma'ana, babu wani mutum da zai zama sarki bayansa.

Idan muka lura da halin da ake ciki yanzu, mun ga cewa Sarkin Puri na yanzu shine Sarki na 19 kuma shi ma ba shi da ɗa. Nuni bayyananne na Mahapurush shine cewa dukkan ayyukan Kalki na kafa Dharma za su faru a lokacin mulkin sarkin yanzu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Adi Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, Shri Divyasingha zai zama Sarkin Puri.

A wani rubutun Malika, ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da mulkin Sarki mai suna Shri Divyasingha Deva ke gudana a Puri, a wancan lokacin dukkan ayyukan Ubangiji Kalki za su faru. A wancan lokacin, Ubangiji zai bar Haikalinsa na Jagannath kuma zai haihu a jikin ɗan adam a matsayin Kalki Avatar.

Sannan a cikin rubutun Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, Achyutanandaji ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, “Baalak-Briddha” zai zama Sarkin Puri; wato, mutum zai zama sarki wanda ya hau gadon sarauta tun yana yaro kuma ya ci gaba da kasancewa a wannan matsayi har zuwa tsufa. A lokacin mulkinsa, abubuwa kamar bala'in ruwa da bala'in iska za su faru.

An san cewa a cikin sarakunan da aka yi har yanzu, babu wanda sai Shri Divyasingha Deva ya kasance sarki tun daga yaro har zuwa tsufa. Sarkin yanzu Shri Divyasingha Deva ya hau gadon sarauta a ranar 7 ga Yuli, 1970, yana ɗan shekara 17 kacal. Yayin da yake a wannan matsayi, ya shiga tsufa kuma a halin yanzu yana kimanin shekaru 72. Ta hanyar wannan aya, a cikin harshen alama, Mahapurush ya bayyana cewa Divyasingha Deva zai zama sarki a ƙarshen Kaliyuga.

Achyutananda Dasji ya ƙara rubuta a cikin ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ nasa:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da Shri Divyasingha Deva ke Sarkin Puri, a lokacin wani tauraro mai wutsiya zai bayyana a gabas. Za a yi ruwan taurari a faɗin Duniya. Dabbobin nama kamar karnuka da kyarkeci za su bar dazuzzuka su nufi wuraren zama. Uwar Kali da abokan aikinta Yoginis za su halaka masu zunubi a Duniya, wanda zai kai ga mutuwar mutane marasa adadi.

Mahapurush ya sanar a taƙaice ta hanyar ayar da ke sama cewa abubuwan kafa Dharma za su faru a lokacin mulkin Sarkin Puri mai suna Shri Divyasingha Deva a ƙarshen Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush ya ce wa almajirinsa Ramadas a wani rubutun Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, Ya almajiri Ramadas, saurari wane zamani nake magana a kai—a lokacin da waɗannan abubuwan suka faru, mulkin Sarki mai suna Divyasingha zai kasance yana gudana, wanda ba zai sami ɗa ba; 'ya'ya mata kawai.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin ɗaya daga cikin rubutunsa na Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, za a sami mulkin Sarki mai suna Divyasingha wanda ba zai sami ɗa ba.

Wannan annabcin na Mahapurush ya dace da Sarkin Puri na yanzu, Shri Divyasingha Deva, gaba ɗaya. Sarkin yanzu ba shi da ɗa; yana da 'ya'ya mata huɗu kawai.

A wani rubutunsa na Malika, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta kalmomin da Ubangiji Krishna ya faɗa wa Arjuna a Dwaparayuga, inda Ubangiji Krishna ya ce:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, Sarkin Puri ba zai yi hidimata ta yau da kullun ba (a cikin siffar Jagannath).

Achyutananda Dasji sannan ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Fassarar Harafi:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, Sarkin Puri ba zai zauna a Shrikshetra ko Puri ba, amma zai zauna a wani wuri dabam. Ba zai auri gimbiya daga gidan sarautar Odisha ba amma zai auri gimbiya daga wata jiha dabam.

Wani babban waliyi na Odisha da aka haifa a ƙarni na 18, Mahapurush Arakshita Das, ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, Divyasingha Deva zai zama Sarkin Puri kuma ba zai zauna a Shrikshetra ko Puri ba, amma zai zauna a yankin Khandagiri ko Bhubaneswar. Mahapurush ya ƙara rubuta yadda shi da kansa zai iya sanin sirrin waɗannan al'amura; Uwar Farko (Adi Mata) ce ta faɗa masa waɗannan abubuwan.

Bari in sanar da masu karatu cewa dukkan sarakunan da suka gabaci Sarkin yanzu sun kasance suna zaune a Puri, Odisha, kuma suna yin hidimar Ubangiji Jagannath ta yau da kullun, amma Sarkin yanzu Shri Divyasingha Deva yana yin hidimar Ubangiji Jagannath ne kawai a wasu lokuta na musamman. Ba ya zaune a Puri amma yana zaune a Shahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar. Bugu da ƙari, Shri Divyasingha Deva bai auri gimbiya daga Odisha ba amma ya auri 'yar Lieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak na Jammu.

Annabce-annabcen uku da ke sama sun dace da Sarkin Puri na yanzu, Shri Divyasingha Deva, gaba ɗaya. Wannan kuma hujja ce cewa Kaliyuga zai ƙare a lokacin mulkin Sarki Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a wani rubutunsa na Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da Divyasingha Deva ke Sarkin Puri, to mulkin Ananta Keshari, wato, Ubangiji Kalki, zai faru. Zai zama ranar Magha Saptami; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) zai zama wurin.

Wannan aya kuma ta tabbatar da cewa Sarkin Puri a lokacin ayyukan Ubangiji na Kalki ba zai zama wani ba face Shri Divyasingha Deva.

A cikin rubutunsa na Malika mai suna ‘Mohakalpa’, Achyutanandaji ya ba da wata alama da ke nuna sarai cewa Shri Divyasingha Deva zai zama sarki na ƙarshe na Puri. Mahapurush ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, Sarkin Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zai bar addininsa na Vaishnava ya rungumi aikin Kshatriya kuma zai yi yaƙe-yaƙe da yawa. Da zarar ya ga Ubangiji Kalki, zai fadi a ƙafafun Ubangiji, ya karɓi albarkar Ubangiji, kuma ya halaka 'yan bidi'a lakh tara. A yankin Jagannath, Puri, za a gwada dukkan Brahmins da ke wajen da Gayatri Mantra. Saboda tsoron zunubin kashe Brahmin, Divyasingha Deva ba zai kashe Brahmins da suka kasa gwajin ba. Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa dukkaninsu za su halaka ta hanyar Sudarshana Chakra na Ubangiji da kansa. Ubangiji zai sanya Sudarshana Chakra nasa a sararin sama kuma ya yanke kan Brahmins da wannan chakra waɗanda ba su karanta Gayatri Mantra ba.

A nan, Mahapurush ya ba da hujja cewa Sarkin Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zai haɗu da Ubangiji Kalki kuma zai ba da gudummawa ga kafa Dharma. A bayyane yake cewa ayyukan Ubangiji na halaka na Ubangiji Kalki za su faru a lokacin mulkin Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Sannan Mahapurush ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika mai suna ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da sojojin waje suka kama Sarkin Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, a matsayin fursuna, to bayan kwana biyu, Ubangiji Kalki zai isa wurin sarki da daddare, ya 'yantar da shi daga bauta, kuma ya ɓoye shi a ƙafafun dutsen Khandagiri.

Bugu da ƙari, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika mai suna ‘Padmakalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Fassarar Harafi:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da babban bala'in ruwa ya faru, masu bauta na Ubangiji Kalki za su kasance lafiya a cikin kogon Ananta da ke kan dutsen Khandagiri. A wancan lokacin, gwamnati za ta samar da abinci da kayan agaji ga waɗanda abin ya shafa. Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa a wancan lokacin, Ananta Madhava, wato, Ubangiji Kalki, zai kasance a Khandagiri, Odisha. Sarki Divyasingha Deva ma zai kasance tare da Shi.

Mahapurush ya sake rubutawa a cikin rubutunsa na Malika mai suna ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, lokacin da Ubangiji Ananta Keshari, wato, Ubangiji Kalki, yake a Khandagiri, to a wancan lokacin Divyasingha Deva ma zai kasance a Khandagiri. A Khandagiri, Divyasingha Deva zai sami hangen nesa (Darshan) na Ubangiji.

Daga dukkan waɗannan ayoyi uku da aka bayar a sama ma, an san cewa a lokacin ayyukan Ubangiji na Kalki, Sarkin Puri zai zama Divyasingha Deva.

6.1 Haɗuwar Ubangiji Kalki da Baladeva tare da Divyasingha Deva da Sarakuna da yawa

Bhavishya Malika cike take da abubuwan ban mamaki da annabce-annabce masu yawa. Daga cikin waɗannan annabce-annabce akwai hasashen sarakuna da yawa da za su haɗu da Ubangiji.

Mahapurush ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika cewa a nan gaba, wani lokaci zai zo inda sojojin waje za su mamaye daga Chhatia, Jajpur zuwa Nilagiri ko Nilachal, Puri. Har ma babban sojan abokan gaba zai shiga cikin Haikalin Jagannath. Ganin duk wannan cikin matsanancin damuwa, Ubangiji, wanda ya nutsu cikin tuba, zai tuna Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, da Shalya. Da zarar Ubangiji ya tuna su, tunani da tuban waɗannan jarumai huɗu, ƙwararru a harbin kibiya, za su karye, kuma dukkan su huɗu za su isa Chhatia kuma su fadi a ƙafafun Ubangiji, suna neman umarninsa. Ubangiji zai umarci su huɗu su halaka babban sojan Mughal kuma su kare masu bauta. Bisa ga umarnin Ubangiji, su huɗu za su halaka sojojin Mughal sannan su koma wurin Ubangiji. A wancan lokacin, masu bauta marasa adadi za su kasance tare da Ubangiji a Chhatia, kuma na tsawon kwanaki 27, Chhatia za ta zama wurin haɗuwar masu bauta. A wannan lokacin, sarakunan Rasha, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, da sauransu, za su haɗu da Ubangiji, su karɓi Darshan nasa, kuma su miƙa kansu a ƙafafunsa. A wancan lokacin, Ubangiji zai ɗauki dukkan su kuma ya isa Sealdah don neman Ubangiji Baladeva don halaka sauran sojojin waje. Ta wannan hanyar, dukkan sarakunan da ke tare da Ubangiji za su je Sealdah kuma su haɗu da Baladeva, inda Arjuna da Bhima za su riga sun kasance.

A nan Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa a wannan lokacin, daga cikin sarakunan da za su haɗu da Ubangiji Ananta Keshari da Ubangiji Baladeva, Sarkin Puri, Shrikshetra, ma zai kasance a wurin. Yana bayyana wanda zai kasance, Mahapurush ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Fassarar Harafi:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, daga cikin waɗannan sarakunan da za su karɓi Darshan na Ubangiji zai kasance Sarkin Shrikshetra Puri, Divyasingha Deva.

Daga tattaunawar da aka yi a sama, a bayyane yake cewa Sarkin Puri a lokacin ayyukan Ubangiji na Kalki zai kasance Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Daga ayoyi 17 da aka ambata a sama, an tabbatar da cewa a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, lokacin da Ubangiji Kalki ya haifu kuma lokacin da ayyukan Ubangiji na halaka suka faru a duk faɗin duniya, lokacin da halakar miyagu, ceton masu bauta, da aikin kafa Dharma suka faru, a wancan lokacin wani sarki mai suna Divyasingha Deva zai zauna a kan kursiyin Jagannath Puri, kuma shi ma zai zama sarki na ƙarshe da zai ci gaba da kasancewa a wannan matsayi.

Shri Divyasingha Deva zai zama sarki na ƙarshe—wannan Mahapurush Jagannath Das, ɗaya daga cikin Panchasakhas, ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika, wanda muka ambata a farkon ayar wannan babi.

Amma abin lura shi ne cewa ba Jagannath Das kaɗai ba, har ma wani Mahapurush daga cikin Panchasakhas, Shishu Ananta Das, ya rubuta game da wannan batu a cikin rubutunsa na Malika mai suna ‘Patta Madana’. Mahapurush ya rubuta:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Ma'ana:
Ma'ana, Sarki Divyasingha Deva zai yi mulki kuma bayan shi, ba za a sake samun mulkin wani sarki ba. Divyasingha Deva zai zama sarki na ƙarshe. A wasu kalmomi, Kaliyuga za ta ƙare a lokacinsa, kuma ayyukan Ubangiji na halaka na Ubangiji Kalki za su faru.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Abin lura a nan shi ne cewa Mahapurush bai tsaya kawai da cewa sunan Sarkin Puri a ƙarshen Kaliyuga zai zama Divyasingha Deva ba; a maimakon haka, ya faɗi abubuwa da yawa game da shi, kamar zai zama sarki na 19, zai zama Divyasingha Deva na huɗu, ba zai sami ɗa ba, zai sami 'ya'ya mata kawai, ba zai zauna a Puri ba amma a Bhubaneswar, ba zai iya yin ayyukan yau da kullun na Ubangiji Jagannath ba, zai hau sarauta tun yana yaro kuma ya ci gaba da zama sarki har zuwa tsufa, da sauransu.

Dukkan waɗannan abubuwa sun dace da Sarki Shri Divyasingha Deva na yanzu daidai.

7 Alamomi na Ƙarshen Kaliyuga a Haikalin Jagannath

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das da sauran Panchasakhas sun rubuta a wurare da yawa a cikin rubutunsu na Malika cewa masu bauta za su sami alamomi da yawa daga Haikalin Jagannath ta hanyar da za su iya sanin cewa Kali Yuga ta ƙare kuma Ubangiji Kalki ya bayyana. Waɗannan alamomi za a gan su ba kawai daga Haikalin Jagannath ba har ma daga sauran gidajen ibada da haikalai a Indiya, amma da farko za a gan su daga Haikalin Jagannath da ke Puri.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika cewa a ƙarshen Kali Yuga, za a ga nau'ikan abubuwan da ba su dace ba (alamomi marasa kyau) akai-akai a cikin Shrimandir (Haikalin Jagannath) da ke Puri. Ya ƙara da cewa wannan maganata za ta tabbata gaskiya.

7.1 Tarihin Alamomi Marasa Kyau a Haikalin Jagannath

A yau mun ga cewa a cikin shekaru 15-20 da suka gabata, an shaida alamomi marasa kyau da yawa a Haikalin Jagannath, kamar babban dutse da ya faɗo daga "Amla Bedha" na haikalin a shekarar 1990; duwatsu da suka faɗo ƙasa daga manyan gine-ginen haikalin, ciki har da wurin tsarki, sau da yawa a cikin 'yan shekarun nan; tsuntsaye masu cin mushe (shirwa) suna zama a kan sandar tutar haikalin da Aruna Stambha a gaban Singhadwara (Ƙofar Zaki); shirwa marasa adadi suna shawagi a saman haikalin; alfarwa mai ado da duwatsu masu daraja (Ratnachandua) da aka sanya a kan alloli ta kama wuta; 'Patitapabana Bana' (tuta) da aka ɗaga a saman haikalin ta kama wuta; Neelachakra mai nauyin tan 2.2, wanda aka yi da Ashtadhatu (ƙarfe takwas), ya lanƙwasa saboda iska mai ƙarfi; 'Patitapabana Bana' ta tashi da iska kuma ta faɗa cikin teku a lokacin babban guguwa; reshe na babban Kalpavata (bishiyar banyan) da ke cikin harabar haikalin ya karye; Patitapabana Bana ta ƙone saboda walƙiya; kimanin murhu na ƙasa 40 da 'yan iska suka lalata a cikin kicin mai tsarki na haikalin; an sami fashe-fashe a Aruna Stambha na haikalin, katako a cikin Natamandapa, zauren da ke gaban wurin tsarki (Jagamohana), da kusa da Singhadwara; dutsen 'Padma Pakhuda' (furen lotus) ya ɓace daga Ratnasinghasana (kursiyin mai ado da duwatsu masu daraja); walƙiya ta faɗa a kicin na haikalin; sata da ke faruwa a haikalin da daddare; karuwar beraye a haikalin saboda raguwar adadin masu bauta a lokacin annobar Corona, wanda ya haifar da damuwa da kuma kawo cikas ga al'adun haikalin; an sami tabo na jini a wurare da yawa ciki har da Ratnasinghasana na haikalin, sannan aka yi babban wanka na al'ada (Mahasnana) na Chaturdha Murti (alloli huɗu); sararin sama ya koma ja a kusa da haikalin; bacewar jemagu ba zato ba tsammani waɗanda a baya an gan su da yawa a haikalin; amfani da wayoyin hannu duk da cewa an hana su a haikalin; yaudarar bidiyo a cikin harabar haikalin ta amfani da kyamarorin leƙen asiri da aka ɓoye a cikin tabarau; shigar waɗanda ba Hindu ba da waɗanda ba Sanatanis ba cikin haikalin duk da haramcin; kashe kai ko mutuwar da ke faruwa saboda wasu dalilai a cikin haikalin; mutuwar a Baisi Pahacha, wato, matakai 22 na haikalin; an sami fashe-fashe a Gumuta (dome) na Hasti Dwara (Ƙofar Arewa) na haikalin, da sauransu.

An sami annabce-annabce daban-daban game da wasu daga cikin waɗannan abubuwan a cikin rubutun Malika daban-daban, inda aka bayyana sarai cewa waɗannan abubuwan za su faru ne kawai a ƙarshen Kali Yuga kuma za su ba masu bauta alamar saukowar Ubangiji Kalki a duniya. Saboda haka, waɗanda ke nuna shakku da rashin imani a yau game da ƙarshen Kali Yuga da bayyanar Ubangiji Kalki ya kamata su sake nazarin ra'ayoyinsu.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin 'Jaiphula Malika' nasa:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa a lokacin da aka ambata, abubuwan da suka shafi faɗuwar duwatsu akai-akai a Haikalin Jagannath da ke Puri za su bayyana, waɗanda masu bauta za su gani sarai da idanunsu.

Idan muka yi la'akari da gaskiyar wannan annabci, za mu ga cewa bayan wani dutse mai nauyin tan 5-6 ya faɗo daga "Amla Bedha" na haikalin a ranar 14 ga Yuni, 1990, irin waɗannan abubuwan sun faru sau da yawa. A ranar 13 ga Agusta, 1992, manyan duwatsu biyu sun faɗo kai tsaye cikin wurin tsarki na haikalin. Haka kuma, a ranar 26 ga Oktoba, 1998, wani dutse mai nauyin kilo 15 ya birgima daga babban haikalin kuma ya faɗo kusa da haikalin Bimala a cikin harabar babban haikalin. A ranar 1 ga Nuwamba, 2011, wani dutse mai nauyin kimanin tan 1 ya faɗo daga kusa da Dadhinauti na ƙofar yamma ta Haikalin Jagannath.

Haka kuma a shekarar 2015, a ranar 2 ga Fabrairu, wani dutse mai nauyin kilo 30-40 ya faɗo ƙasa daga Jalakrida Mandapa da aka gina kusa da Haikalin Bimala a yammacin Haikalin Jagannath. Da yake magana game da kwanan nan, a ranar 3 ga Agusta, 2022, wani yanki na filasta mai nauyin kimanin kilo 1.5 ya faɗo daga rufin ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki na wannan haikali da aka gina a ƙarni na goma sha biyu. A ranar 23 ga Oktoba, 2022, wani dutse ya faɗo daga kusurwar kudu maso gabas na haikalin kusa da Majana Mandapa da Charcha Ghara, kuma cikin mako guda bayan haka, a ranar 28 ga Oktoba, 2022, wani dutse mai nauyin kilo 2-3 ya faɗo daga mataki na uku sama da mutum-mutumin Sakhi kusa da wurin haɗin Natamandapa da Jagamohana. Har ma a shekarar 2024, a ranar 9 ga Maris, wani dutse mai nauyin kimanin kilo huɗu ya faɗo daga rufin Haikalin Baraha da ke gaban Haikalin Bimala a harabar Haikalin Jagannath.

Ta haka, annabcin da Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya yi ƙarnuka da suka wuce game da duwatsu da ke faɗowa "tharaku thara" (akai-akai) daga Haikalin Jagannath ya tabbata gaskiya a cikin ƴan shekarun nan.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya tattauna dalla-dalla alamomi masu yawa na ƙarshen Kali Yuga da aka samu daga Haikalin Jagannath a Puri a cikin rubuce-rubucen Malika da yawa ciki har da 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', da 'Chakada Madana'. A cikin wannan mahallin, ya rubuta cewa lokacin da dutse ya faɗo daga "Amla Bedha" na Haikalin Jagannath, masu ibada su fahimci cewa ƙarshen Kali Yuga yana kusa sosai kuma bala'o'i da masifu da yawa za su faɗa wa duniya a nan gaba. Ya ce faɗuwar wannan dutse zai zama muhimmiyar alama ta sirri ta ƙarshen Kali Yuga, kuma shekaru 23 bayan faɗuwarsa, Kali Yuga zai ƙare. Ya ƙara da cewa a lokacin da aka ambata, wani tsuntsu mai nuna alamar bala'i zai zauna a saman Haikalin Jagannath da Aruna Stambha da ke gaban Singhadwara na haikalin. Ba wai kawai wannan ba, Neelachakra mai nauyin tan 2.2 da aka yi da Ashtadhatu a saman haikalin zai lanƙwasa saboda saurin iska mai ƙarfi.

Masu karatu za su yi mamaki sanin cewa duk annabce-annabcen da aka ambata a sama da aka rubuta ƙarnuka da suka wuce sun tabbata a halin yanzu a Haikalin Jagannath. A cikin ruwan sama mai yawa, da farko, a ranar 14 ga Yuni, 1990, wani dutse mai nauyin tan 5-6 ya faɗo daga "Amla Bedha" na haikalin. Sannan kuma an ga wani tsuntsu mai nuna alamar bala'i zaune a kan Aruna Stambha.

Abin lura ne cewa ba a taɓa ganin wani tsuntsu yana tashi a kan Haikalin Jagannath Puri ba, kuma masana kimiyya da masu bincike sun kasa nazarin dalilin da ya sa hakan ya faru. Amma a wannan lokacin ƙarshen Kali Yuga, da yake tabbatar da annabce-annabcen Malika gaskiya, an ga tsuntsaye masu nuna alamar bala'i kamar shirgi/ungulu suna zaune a kan Patitapabana Bana (sandar tuta) a saman haikalin sau da yawa. A cikin ƴan shekarun nan, hakan ya faru a ranar 11 ga Agusta, 2018, 26 ga Disamba, 2018, da 23 ga Yuli, 2020, lokacin da aka ga tsuntsaye masu nuna alamar bala'i suna zaune a kan sandar tutar haikalin. A ranar 7 ga Disamba, 2021, an ga tsuntsaye marasa adadi suna shawagi a cikin garke a kan Haikalin Jagannath. An sake ganin irin wannan lamari a ranar 17 ga Agusta, 2022. Wannan jerin abubuwan bai tsaya a nan ba; a watannin Mayu, Yuni, da Satumba na shekarar 2023, mazauna Puri sun sake shaida wannan lamari mara daɗi lokacin da aka ga irin waɗannan tsuntsaye suna zaune a kan sandar tutar da aka sanya a saman haikalin.

Kwanan nan a shekarar 2025 ma, an ga wani tsuntsu a saman Aruna Stambha a ranar 4 ga Satumba, yayin da aka ga shirgi suna zaune a saman haikalin a ranar 2 ga Satumba, 16 ga Satumba, da 14 ga Oktoba. A ranar 9 ga Disamba, an ga tsuntsaye marasa adadi suna shawagi a kusa da Shrimandir.

Bugu da ƙari ga duk wannan, mummunar guguwar iska, 'Fani', wadda ta afkawa gabar tekun Puri a ranar 3 ga Mayu, 2019, ta lanƙwasa Neelachakra da ke saman haikalin, ta haka ta cika wani annabci da aka rubuta ƙarnuka da suka wuce a cikin wannan jerin.

Abin lura ne cewa waɗannan annabce-annabcen da aka rubuta a ƙarni na 16 ba alamomi ne kawai na ƙarshen Kali Yuga ba, amma kuma alamomi ne da Panchasakhas suka bayar na zuwan Kalki Avatar, saboda Kalki Avatar zai zo ne kawai a ƙarshen Kali Yuga, kamar yadda duk littattafai suka bayyana ciki har da Bhavishya Malika.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya ambaci wata alama ta ƙarshen Kali Yuga da haihuwar Ubangiji Kalki a cikin rubuce-rubucen Malika nasa 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' da 'Kalikalpa Gita'. Ya rubuta cewa wani lokaci zai zo inda Patitapabana Bana da aka haɗa a saman Neelachakra na Shrimandir zai tashi daga wurinsa. Mahapurush ya kuma rubuta cewa tutar za ta tashi ta faɗa cikin teku.

Wannan annabci da Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya yi shi ma ya tabbata gaskiya. Kwana ɗaya kafin mummunar guguwar iska 'Fani' ta afkawa gabar tekun Puri, a ranar 2 ga Mayu, 2019, saboda iska mai ƙarfi, Patitapabana Bana ya tashi ya faɗa cikin teku. Daidai irin wannan lamari ya sake faruwa a ranar 18 ga Mayu, 2020, kwana biyu kafin guguwar 'Amphan' ta afkawa gabar tekun Odisha.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Ma'ana:
Da yake ambato kalmomin Ubangiji, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' cewa lokacin da Ubangiji Jagannath ya bar Nilachala, wato, Shrimandir, don haihuwa a jikin mutum, a lokacin rumfa mai ado da duwatsu masu daraja da aka sanya a saman alloli uku masu tsarki a Haikalin Jagannath zai kama wuta.

Abin lura ne cewa lamarin Ratnachandua da ya kama wuta ya faru a shekarar 2004-05, kuma ta haka wannan annabci na Mahapurush ya tabbata gaskiya.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Ma'ana:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' shekaru 600 da suka wuce cewa wani lokaci zai zo inda reshen Kalpavata da ke cikin harabar Haikalin Jagannath zai karye. Har ma ya rubuta a wani rubutunsa cewa lokacin da akwai 'tara' kusa da 'ɗaya', wato, lambar 19, to a lokacin ne kawai wannan lamari zai faru.

Daidai da bayanin Mahapurush, wannan annabci ya tabbata gaskiya a shekarar 2019 lokacin da mummunar guguwar iska 'Fani', wadda ta afkawa gabar tekun Puri a ranar 3 ga Mayu, ta lalata babban Kalpavata mai shekaru da yawa da ke cikin harabar haikalin.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Ma'ana:
Daga cikin alamomi da yawa da Mahapurush ya ambata za a samu daga Haikalin Jagannath a Puri a ƙarshen Kali Yuga, ɗaya shine ganin ɗigon jini a kan Ratnasinghasana na gumaka masu tsarki.

Wannan annabci da Mahapurush ya rubuta shi ma ya tabbata gaskiya. A cikin ƴan shekarun nan, ɗigon jini ya faɗo a wurare da yawa a cikin haikalin, ciki har da Ratnasinghasana, bayan haka, bisa ga al'adar haikalin, an tsarkake Chaturdha Murti ta hanyar al'adar Mahasnana (wanka mai girma). Misali, a ranar 6 ga Maris, 2021, an ga jini kusa da 'Bhitar Katha' da ke ɗan nesa daga ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki na haikalin. A ranar 18 ga Afrilu, 2021, saboda faɗa tsakanin ƴan sanda biyu da aka tura don tsaron haikalin, ɗigon jini ya faɗo a harabar haikalin. A ranar 7 ga Maris, 2022, a lokacin 'Abakasha Niti' (al'ada), an sami ɗigon jini kusa da 'Bhitar Katha' a cikin Jagamohana na haikalin (zauren da ke gaban ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki). Sannan a ranar 11 ga Afrilu, 2022, an ga ɗigon jini kusa da Dhukudi Dwara na haikalin. A ranar 3 ga Nuwamba, 2022, bayan al'adun 'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', da 'Tadapa Lagi', an ga ɗigon jini kusa da 'Bhitar Katha'. An sake ganin irin wannan lamari a mako na uku na Janairu a shekarar 2023. Bayan kammala al'adun 'Abakasha' da 'Besa Lagi' na Ubangiji Jagannath, an ga tabo na jini a Shrimandir, saboda haka hadaya (Bhoga) ga Gopalaballabha ba ta cika ba. Sannan a ranar 4 ga Yuni, 2023, bayan kammala wanka mai tsarki (Devasnana), an ga ɗigon jini a kan Snana Mandapa. Har ma kwanan nan, a ranar 13 ga Oktoba, 2023, saboda mummunar faɗa tsakanin masu hidima biyu game da rarraba Prasad nan da nan bayan 'Madhyahna Dhupa' a Shrimandir, ɗigon jini ya faɗo a ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki na haikalin. A shekarar 2024 ma, a safiyar ranar 23 ga Afrilu, bayan buɗe Singhadwara, wata mata daga dangin mai hidima ta sanya hannunta a kan ƙofar haikalin lokacin da yatsanta ya murƙushe a ciki. Sakamakon haka, jini ya fito daga yatsanta kusa da Dhukudi Dwara kusa da Bedha na ciki. A cikin irin wannan yanayi, an yi Mahasnana na Ubangiji Maɗaukaki har ma kafin Mangala Alati. Haka kuma, lamuran zubar jini a harabar haikalin sun ci gaba a shekarar 2025 ma.

7.2 Faɗuwar gumaka na Ubangiji Balabhadra a lokacin Rath Yatra 2024.

A lokacin Rath Yatra na kwanaki 11 a Shri Jagannath Puri, al'adun 'Adapa Mandapa Bije' sun fara da ƙarfe 7 na yamma a ranar 9 ga Yuli, 2024. A lokacin wannan, yayin da ake ɗaukar gumaka na Ubangiji Balabhadra daga karusa zuwa Adapa Mandapa na Haikalin Gundicha, Ya faɗi fuska ƙasa a kan Charamala, saboda haka masu hidima 12 ma sun ji rauni. Irin wannan lamari mara daɗi ya faru a karo na farko a tarihi. Faɗuwar Ubangiji Balabhadra a wannan hanya tabbas alama ce ta bala'o'i masu zuwa, wanda aka ambata kamar haka a cikin rubutun 'Niladri' da ke da alaƙa da dokoki da manufofin Shrikhetra, kamar yadda Ubangiji Brahma da Ubangiji Vishnu suka umarta.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Fassarar Harafi:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Ma'ana:
Wato, "Ya Sarki, idan ba da gangan ba Jagannath, Balabhadra, ko Subhadra suka faɗi, to babban masifa za ta faɗa wa sarki da jihar. Za a sami yunwa, kuma annoba za ta sake afkawa akai-akai."

Wuta a ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki na Haikalin Mahakal, Ujjain a shekarar 2024

A safiyar Litinin, 25 ga Maris, 2024, a lokacin bikin Holi, wata wuta ta tashi a ɗakin tsarki mafi tsarki na Haikalin Mahakaleshwar a Ujjain saboda jefa 'gulal' (foda mai launi) a lokacin Bhasma Aarti. Kimanin mutane 14, ciki har da babban firist da sauran masu hidima, sun sami rauni na ƙonawa a cikin wannan lamari.

7.3 Babban alamomi marasa kyau da aka samu daga Haikalin Jagannath a shekarar 2025

Alamomi marasa kyau da aka samu daga Haikalin Jagannath sun ci gaba a shekarar 2025 ma. Ta hanyar alamomi da yawa, Ubangiji Jagannath ya ba masu ibada nasa alamar bala'o'i masu zuwa, wasu daga cikin manyan alamomin da muke gabatar muku.

(a) Karya Akshaya Danda a ranar 14 ga Fabrairu

Kamar kowace rana, a ranar 14 ga Fabrairu, 2025 (Jumma'a), lokacin da mai hidima Chunara ya isa Neelachakra da ke saman haikalin don canza Patitapabana Bana ko babban tuta, ya sami Akshaya Danda (sandar bamboo da ke riƙe da Patitapabana Bana) a karye. Bayan haka, masu hidima sun ɗaure tutar zuwa Neelachakra da kanta.

Karya Akshaya Danda a wannan hanya ya cutar da tunanin masu ibada marasa adadi, kuma sun nuna baƙin ciki mai girma game da shi. Tare da wannan, masu ibada da yawa sun kira shi alama ce ta bala'o'i masu zuwa a nan gaba.

(b) An sami ɗigon jini a cikin haikalin a ranar 16 ga Fabrairu; An yi Mahasnana na alloli

A ranar 16 ga Fabrairu (Lahadi), bayan kammala al'adun safiya kamar 'Dwara Phita Niti' da 'Mangala Alati', da zaran Darshan na Ubangiji Jagannath ya fara, ba da jimawa ba, masu hidima sun sami tabo na jini kusa da yankin 'Bhitar Katha'. Nan da nan bayan faruwar lamarin, an shirya 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' na gumaka, kuma an dakatar da Darshan na wani lokaci.

(c) Shirin tsuntsu yana tashi da Patitapabana Bana a ranar 13 ga Afrilu

A ranar 13 ga Afrilu, 2025, masu ibada na Ubangiji Jagannath da ke Puri Shrimandir sun ga wata alama da ba a taɓa gani ba. Misalin karfe 5 na yamma, an ga wani shirin tsuntsu yana shawagi a kusa da haikalin yana ɗauke da Patitapabana Bana a bakinsa. A cewar rahotanni, shirin tsuntsun ya zagaya haikalin sau 4-5 sannan ya tashi zuwa teku ta kan ƙofar yamma.

Bidiyon wannan lamari ya yadu a kafofin sada zumunta. Masu ibada da yawa sun nuna baƙin cikinsu da damuwarsu game da wannan. Masu ibada da yawa sun danganta shi da bala'o'i masu zuwa, kuma da yawa sun kira shi wata alama mara kyau da Ubangiji Jagannath ya bayar.

(d) An sami tabo na jini a haikalin a ranar 21 ga Afrilu kuma an yi Mahasnana na gumaka

A ranar 21 ga Afrilu (Litinin) ma, an ga tabo na jini kusa da Bhitar Katha a Shrimandir, bayan haka an yi Mahasnana na gumakan Ubangiji yadda ya kamata bisa ga al'ada. A halin yanzu, an katse Darshan na Ubangiji Maɗaukaki ga masu ibada na kimanin awa 1. A cewar rahotanni, an sami tabo na jini a cikin haikalin saboda gutsuttsarin gilashi da suka soki ƙafar ɗan sanda.

(e) Shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a kan Patitapabana Bana a ranar 2 ga Satumba

An ga wani shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a kan Patitapabana Bana a saman Shrimandir sau da yawa a cikin 'yan shekarun nan. Duk lokacin da aka ga irin wannan lamari, ana nuna shi a kusan dukkan tashoshin labarai na gida a Odisha. A ranar 2 ga Satumba, daidai abin ya faru kuma lokacin da aka fara ganin wani shirin tsuntsu yana shawagi kusa da Neelachakra kuma bayan wani lokaci aka same shi yana zaune a kan Patitapabana Bana da aka haɗa a saman Neelachakra.

(f) Shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a kan Aruna Stambha kuma a ranar 4 ga Satumba

Ba wai Haikalin Jagannath kawai ba, Bhavishya Malika kuma ya bayyana zama na shirin tsuntsu ko wani tsuntsu mai muni a kan Aruna Stambha da ke gaban Singhadwara na haikalin a matsayin alama mara kyau. A ranar 4 ga Satumba, wato, kwana biyu kacal bayan an ga shirin tsuntsu a saman Shrimandir a ranar 2 ga Satumba, an kuma ga wani shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a kan Aruna Stambha. Mutane sun ɗauki wannan ma a matsayin alama mara kyau. Ta hanyar wannan lamari, da yawa sun bayyana annabcin Malika da aka rubuta shekaru 600 da suka wuce a matsayin gaskiya.

(g) Shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a saman haikalin kuma a ranar 16 ga Satumba

Cikin kwanaki 15 kacal na 2 ga Satumba, da sanyin safiyar 16 ga Satumba, an sake ganin wani shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a saman Patitapabana Bana na haikalin.

(h) An sami shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a kan Patitapabana Bana na Shrimandir kuma a ranar 14 ga Oktoba

An sami shirin tsuntsu yana zaune a saman haikalin sau biyu a watan Satumba. Jerin bai tsaya a nan ba; mazauna Puri sun sake ganin maimaita wannan lamari a ranar 14 ga Oktoba. Yawancin masu ibada sun bayyana maimaita zaman shirin tsuntsu a saman haikalin a matsayin alamar bala'o'i masu zuwa.

(i) Shirin tsuntsaye marasa adadi suna shawagi a saman Shrimandir a ranar 9 ga Disamba

Kamar yadda a ranar 7 ga Disamba, 2021, da 17 ga Agusta, 2022, an ga wata alama mara kyau sosai a ranar 9 ga Disamba, 2025, lokacin da aka sami shirin tsuntsaye marasa adadi suna shawagi a saman Haikalin Jagannath. Wannan lamari ya jawo hankalin ba kawai masu ibada a Puri ko Odisha ba, har ma da waɗanda ke zaune a wajen Odisha. Masu ibada da yawa sun kira wannan lamari alamar wani babban abin da ba a so zai faru a nan gaba.

7.4 Alamomin da ba su dace ba da aka samu daga wasu muhimman gidajen ibada da haikalai a shekarar 2025

Wuta ta tashi a harabar Haikalin Mahakaleshwar a Ujjain

A ranar 5 ga Mayu, 2025, da karfe 12 na rana, wuta ta tashi a ɗakin sarrafawa na hukumar kula da gurɓata muhalli da ke kusa da Shankh Dwara a harabar Haikalin Baba Mahakaleshwar da ke Ujjain. Bayan wannan lamari, firgici ya mamaye harabar haikalin. Bayan tashin wutar, an dakatar da shigar masu ibada cikin haikalin na wani lokaci.

Wuta ta tashi a harabar Peetha na Mahapurush Achyutananda Das

A ranar Devasnana Purnima, 11 ga Yuni, 2025, tashin wuta a harabar Nemal Padmabana Peetha na Mahapurush Achyutananda Das da ke Nischintakoili, Cuttack an kuma gan shi a matsayin alama mara kyau sosai ga masu ibada na Malika. Yanayin tsoro ya mamaye taron dubban masu ibada lokacin da fitilar da aka ajiye a harabar ta kama wuta ba zato ba tsammani, kuma cikin ɗan lokaci, harshen wuta mai zafi ya fara tashi.

Gwamnati ta sare bishiyar banyan ta Nemal

A farkon watan Agusta, an sare yawancin kututturen bishiyar banyan mai shekaru da yawa da ke gaban haikalin da ke Sadhana Peetha na babban marubucin rubutun Malika, babban waliyi Shri Achyutananda Das. An yi zargin cewa gwamnati ta ɗauki irin wannan mataki don aikin ci gaban Achyutananda Das Peetha. An sami adawa mai yawa ga wannan daga mazauna gida da firistoci, kuma bayan zanga-zangar su, an bar wasu 'yan rassan bishiyar banyan ba a sare su ba. Ya kamata a lura cewa Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta rubutun Malika yayin da yake zaune a ƙarƙashin 'Bishiyar Banyan ta Nemal' a cikin yanayin tunani. An ga sare bishiyar banyan ta Nemal a matsayin alama mara kyau sosai ga masu ibada na Malika.

Tutar zinariya da aka sanya a saman Haikalin Mahakaleshwar ta faɗi

A ranar 1 ga Yuni, 2025, an sake ganin wata alama mara kyau daga Haikalin Mahakaleshwar da ke Ujjain. A gaskiya, saboda iska mai ƙarfi, tutar zinariya da aka sanya a saman Haikalin Mahakaleshwar ta faɗi ba zato ba tsammani. Firist na haikalin ya bayyana wannan lamari a matsayin abin damuwa daga mahangar addini.

Abin rufe fuska na Ubangiji Shiva da aka yi a kan Shivalinga a Haikalin Mahakaleshwar ya karye ya faɗi

Wani lamari na musamman ya faru a ranar 18 ga Agusta a Haikalin Mahakal da ke Ujjain. A nan, kamar kowace rana da karfe 8:00 na yamma yayin ado (Shringara) na Shivalinga, an yi abin rufe fuska na Ubangiji Shiva da Bhang (man cannabis). An yi amfani da Bhang mai yawa a lokacin. Nan da nan bayan haka, Aarti yana shirin farawa lokacin da Bhang Shringara ya karye ya faɗi. Hanci, leɓɓa, da ido ɗaya sun karye daga abin rufe fuska na Ubangiji Shiva suka faɗi.

An ga wani abin al'ajabi a Haikalin Maa Samaleswari

Daga sanannen haikalin Maa Samaleswari da ke Lakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, a daren Alhamis, 21 ga Agusta, 2025, masu ibada sun sami alamar cewa Allahiyar ta bar gumakinta ta fito. Lokacin da firist ya buɗe ƙofofin haikalin da sanyin safiya don 'Mangala Alati', an ga sawun ƙafa masu fita waje, waɗanda aka zana da Alta (ja mai launi), na Allahiyar, kuma tare da wannan, takobinta (Khanda) da mashi mai kaifi uku (Trishul), waɗanda aka haɗa da gumakan Allahiyar, an same su a gefe.

A cewar 'Bhavishya Malika', a matakin ƙarshe na Kali Yuga, kafin kafa Dharma, dukkan Allahiyoyi za su bar wurarensu su fito don shiga aikin halaka miyagu da masu zunubi. Wannan lamari hujja ce cewa lokacin ya zo kuma wannan annabci yana cika.

Wuta ta tashi a cikin gumaka masu tsarki a Haikalin Jagannath na Brahmagiri

A ranar 28 ga Agusta, 2025, gumakan Ubangiji Jagannath, babban ɗan'uwa Balabhadra, da uwa Subhadra sun kama wuta a Haikalin Jagannath na Brahmagiri da ke ƙauyen Kandagoda a ƙarƙashin gundumar Puri ta Odisha. A gaskiya, bayan kammala ibadar maraice, firistoci sun koma gidajensu. Da misalin karfe 8:30 na dare, saboda hayaki da ke fitowa daga haikalin, lokacin da aka fasa kofar haikalin, an ga cewa gumakan da ke ciki suna cin wuta. Bayan tuntuɓar Muktimandapa, an yi wa Shaligrama ibada tun daga wannan rana. Bayan tattara Daru (itacen tsarki) a Nabakalebara mai zuwa, an sanya sabbin gumaka.

Alamar halaka mai zuwa da aka samu daga Haikalin Lingaraj, sandar tutar haikalin ta karye

A ranar Litinin, 15 ga Satumba, 2025, saboda tsananin ruwan sama da iska, sandar tutar Haikalin Lingaraj da aka gina a ƙarni na 11 da ke Bhubaneswar ta karye. A safiyar Talata, bayan da masu hidima suka gani, an ɗaga tuta a kan sabuwar sanda.

Tutar da ke kan kololuwar Haikalin Lingaraj da ke Bhubaneswar ta yage saboda tsananin ruwan sama da guguwa. Wurin tsarki ya shafa ta hanyar walƙiya; duhu ya mamaye haikalin.

A cikin tsananin ruwan sama da guguwa mai tsanani a ranar Laraba, 8 ga Oktoba, 2025, Haikalin Lingaraj da ke babban birnin Odisha, Bhubaneswar, ya zama wanda walƙiya ta shafa. Wannan ma alama ce ta wani abu mara kyau. Tutar haikalin ta yage saboda guguwar, yayin da kyamarorin CCTV na haikalin da tsarin hasken wuta suka lalace ta hanyar walƙiya. An ruwaito cewa wasu masu ibada da masu hidima da ke cikin haikalin sun kuma sami girgizar lantarki.

(j) A karon farko a cikin tarihinsa na shekaru 500, ba a miƙa Bal Bhog a Haikalin Banke Bihari a Vrindavan ba

Thakur Banke Bihari, wanda ya bayyana kimanin shekaru dari biyar da suka wuce, ana miƙa masa Bhog (hadaya ta abinci) a lokuta huɗu na yini. Kafin a shimfiɗa wurin zama don Darshan na safe, ana miƙa Bal Bhog. Kafin haikalin ya rufe da rana, ana miƙa Raj Bhog, kuma lokacin da kofofin suka buɗe da maraice, ana miƙa Utthapan Bhog. Lokacin da kofofin suka rufe, ana miƙa Shayan Bhog. An ba da kwangilar shirya Bhog ga mai yin kayan zaki (Halwai). Mai yin kayan zaki yana shirya Bhog kuma ya ba shi ga masu hidima, kuma sukan miƙa Bhog.

Amma a ranar 15 ga Disamba da karfe 8:45 na safe, kofofin haikalin sun buɗe, amma mai yin kayan zaki bai zo da Bal Bhog ba. Lokacin da aka tuntube shi, an gano cewa mai yin kayan zaki bai zo ba saboda rashin biyan kuɗin wata-wata. A karon farko a cikin tarihinsa na shekaru 500, irin wannan abu ya faru lokacin da ba a iya miƙa Bal Bhog ga Banke Bihari Ji a wannan rana ba.

7.5 Shirya Rath Yatra a lokacin da bai dace ba

Kamar yadda muka sani duka, ana shirya Rath Yatra kowace shekara a ranar Dwitiya Tithi na Shukla Paksha na watan Ashadha. An bi wannan al'ada koyaushe a sauran haikalin Jagannath a Indiya da ƙasashen waje, ciki har da Haikalin Jagannath a Puri. Amma a cikin 'yan shekarun nan, an sami lokuta da yawa inda aka ga ISKCON tana keta wannan al'ada.

A wannan al'amari, bayan da Babban Mai Gudanarwa na Hukumar Haikalin Shree Jagannath da Shugaban Kwamitin Gudanarwa na Haikalin Shree Jagannath, Gajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, suka nuna adawa, kuma aka rubuta korafi ga hedkwatar ISKCON da ke Mayapur, wasu gyare-gyare sun faru tabbas, amma har yanzu ana ganin ISKCON tana shirya Rath Yatras marasa lokaci a Indiya da kuma musamman a ƙasashen waje.

A cikin wannan jerin, ISKCON kwanan nan ta shirya Rath Yatra a Taiwan a ranar 23 ga Nuwamba, 2025, wanda al'ummar Odia a duk faɗin duniya da kuma masu ibada marasa adadi na Jagannath na Odisha suka soki. Masu ibada sun ɗauke shi ba kawai keta dokokin gargajiya ba har ma sun ɗauke shi a matsayin rashin kula da umarnin Ubangiji Jagannath da kansa.


7.6 Kuskure a kalandar Ingilishi ta 2026 da Hukumar Shrimandir ta buga

Kwanan nan, an gano wani babban kuskure a kalandar Ingilishi ta 2026 da Hukumar Haikalin Jagannath ta fitar. A gaskiya, an buga hoton Ratnabedi a cikin kalandar inda tsarin Triniti bai dace ba. Daga mahangar mai kallo, a hoton kalandar, an sanya Ubangiji Jagannath a gefen hagu, sannan Allah Subhadra, sannan babban ɗan'uwa Balabhadra, yayin da a cikin tsari mai kyau, Ubangiji Balabhadra ya kamata ya kasance a gefen hagu, sannan Allah Subhadra, sannan Shri Jagannath. Wato, kuskuren shine a cikin kalandar, an buga hoton babban ɗan'uwa Balabhadra a madadin Ubangiji Jagannath, kuma hoton Ubangiji Jagannath yana madadin Ubangiji Balabhadra. Bugu da ƙari, ba a ba Chakraraj Sudarshan wuri a hoton Ratnasinghasana a cikin kalandar ba. Ba wai kawai wannan ba, an kuma buga hoton Rath Yatra a cikin kalandar, wanda ke nuna karusar Uwa Subhadra da farko, sannan karusar Ubangiji Jagannath Maɗaukaki, kuma a ƙarshe karusar Ubangiji Balabhadra.

Saboda waɗannan kurakurai, akwai rashin gamsuwa da fushi a zukatan masu ibada. Akwai kuma fushi a tsakanin masu hidima na Haikalin Jagannath game da wannan lamari. A cewar wasu masu ibada, wannan alama ce da Ubangiji Jagannath da kansa ya bayar game da bala'o'i masu zuwa da kuma canji a tsarin duniya a sabuwar shekara.

8 Haihuwar Ubangiji Kalki – Yaushe kuma A Ina?

A cewar Bhavishya Malika, Ubangiji Kalki an riga an haife shi. Ba wai Ubangiji Kalki kaɗai ba, har ma da dukkan jarumai na zamanin Mahabharata kamar Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, da sauransu, duk sun sake haihuwa a wannan duniya. A cewar Malika, a wannan karon duk waɗannan jarumai za su yi yaƙi tare a gefen Dharma da Adharma.

Duk da haka, ba za mu tattauna sauran jarumai a nan ba kuma za mu iyakance maganganunmu ga Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Ubangiji Kalki Ram kawai.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da Patitapabana Bana (tuta) na Haikalin Puri Jagannath ta tashi daga wurinta ta tafi wani wuri, a daidai wannan lokacin, Mahaprabhu Kalki za a haife shi a Jajanagar ko Jajpur na Odisha a gidan wani Brahmin wanda ke rera yabon Ubangiji Vishnu.

Ya kamata in fayyace wa masu karatu cewa an ga wannan lamari sau da yawa daga Haikalin Jagannath, misali a lokacin shekarar 1990-91, a lokacin shekarar 2005, sannan a lokacin Cyclone Fani da Cyclone Amphan a shekarun 2019 da 2020 bi da bi, da sauransu.

Mahapurush ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika cewa haihuwar Mahaprabhu Kalki za ta faru ne kawai bayan wannan lamari. Tun da wannan lamari ya faru, ana iya cewa da tabbaci cewa Ubangiji Kalki an haife shi. Amma tun da wannan lamari ya faru a shekaru daban-daban, ba zai yiwu a kai ga takamaiman shekarar haihuwar Mahaprabhu daga wannan lamari kaɗai ba.

Don sanin a wace shekara aka haifi Mahaprabhu, za mu buƙaci taimakon wasu ayoyi na Malika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Ma'ana:
Ta hanyar ayoyin da ke sama, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya gaya wa almajirinsa Ramadas cewa lokacin da wani sarki mai suna Shri Dibyasingha Deb ke mulki a Puri, a wannan lokacin Ananta Keshari, wato, Mahaprabhu Kalki, zai yi mulkinsa a Ekamra Nagar ko Bhubaneswar. Zai zama rana ta bakwai (Saptami Tithi) na watan Magha. Mahapurush ya ci gaba da cewa a wannan lokacin, ni ma zan zauna tare da Ubangiji kuma in girma. Wannan zai faru ne lokacin da Sarkin Visakhapatnam (wato, Babban Ministan Andhra Pradesh) ya mutu.

Idan muka yi la'akari da kyau, za mu ga cewa ta hanyar alamar, Mahapurush yana magana ne game da mutuwar Babban Ministan Andhra Pradesh, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, wanda ya faru a wani hatsarin jirgin sama mai saukar ungulu a shekarar 2009. Daga ayar da ke sama, Mahapurush ba ya faɗin shekarar haihuwar Ubangiji ba, amma tabbas yana bayyana cewa a kusan shekarar 2009, ayyukan Kalki (leelas) za su ci gaba.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin ‘Jaiphula Malika’ nasa –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Ma'ana:
Wato, Ubangiji ɗaya wanda ya zo a baya a matsayin Shri Krishna da Shri Chaitanya, zai zo duniya a cikin siffar Kalki a lamba 47.

An san cewa wannan lamba 47 mallakin Sarkin Puri ne, Shri Dibyasingha Deb, wanda ke faɗuwa tsakanin shekarun 2005 da 2008.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Ma'ana:
Wato, Mahaprabhu tabbas zai yi bayyanuwa a duniya bayan ya bar mazauninsa na Jagannath. Yayin da yake bayyana shekarar haihuwar Mahaprabhu, Mahapurush ya ce lokacin da ka ƙara ashirin (20) zuwa lambar Revati Nakshatra (27), to lambar da ta fito ya kamata a fahimce ta a matsayin shekarar haihuwar Ubangiji. Wato, 20+27 = 47.

An san cewa wannan lamba ita ce shekarar mulki (Anka) ta Sarkin Puri, Dibyasingha Deb, wanda ke nuni ga ɗaya daga cikin shekarun tsakanin 2005 da 2008.

Mahapurush ya sake rubutawa a cikin ɗaya daga cikin rubutunsa na Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Ma'ana:
Wato, lokacin da ya zama shekara ta 45 ta Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb na Puri, to Ubangiji Shri Hari zai ɗauki bayyanuwar Kalki kuma ya sauka a duniya.

Yana da kyau a lura cewa shekara ta 45 ta Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb na Puri tana nuni zuwa shekara tsakanin 2005 zuwa 2008.

Achyutananda Das Ji ya sake rubutawa a cikin rubutunsa na Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Fassarar Harafi:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Ma'ana:
Wato, a ƙarshen Kaliyuga, za a sami rashin adalci mai yawa a duniya. Ba wanda zai yi la'akari da iyali ko bin Dharma. Za a sami hargitsi ko'ina; ba wanda zai yi kwanakinsa cikin kwanciyar hankali da farin ciki. A wancan lokacin, a shekara ta 45 ta sarkin da ake kira Dibyasingha Deb (tsakanin 2005-2008), Ubangiji Shri Hari zai sauko a wannan duniya mai mutuwa a cikin siffar Kalki. Zai cire nauyin zunubi daga duniya, ya halaka mugaye, kuma ya kawar da 'Mlechhas'.

Mahapurushas sun bayyana haihuwar Ubangiji Kalki a wurare da yawa a cikin rubuce-rubuce masu yawa.

A wani rubutunsa na Malika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Ma'ana:
Wato, Kogin Baitarani yana gudana kusa da Haikalin Biraja wanda yake a gundumar Jajpur ta Odisha. Kusa da Dashashwamedha Ghat na wannan Kogin Baitarani akwai haikalin Saptamatruka, inda Uwayen Allah Bakwai—wato Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, da Narasimhi—suke.

Mahapurush ya rubuta cewa lokacin da jini ya fito daga leɓɓa, wato, bakunan, Uwayen Saptamatruka, zai zama alama wadda masu ibada ya kamata su fahimci cewa Ubangiji Kalki an haife shi ko kuma haihuwarsa tana kusa sosai.

An san cewa wannan lamarin ya riga ya faru a Haikalin Saptamatruka, yana tabbatar da annabcin Mahapurush Achyutananda Das gaskiya.

A cewar firistocin Haikalin Biraja, Jajpur, da Haikalin Saptamatruka, da kuma mutanen yankin, zubar jini daga bakunan Uwayen Saptamatruka ya riga ya faru a shekarar 2005.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ya rubuta a cikin rubutunsa na Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Fassarar Harafi:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Ma'ana:
